LI5 : How does binary work ? I know it 's written in 1s and 0s , but how do you read / write it ? where do you start ? Explain like I'm five.
Perplexity (WM): 8.6900 | Perplexity (No WM): 6.8943
Watermarked Text Detection: True Positive
Non-Watermarked Text Detection: True Negative

[Watermarked Text]
Sure! Imagine you have a special code book that can only use two letters: 1 and 0. This code book is called binary. 

To write a number in binary, you just use 1s and 0s instead of the digits we use. For example, the number 5 would be written as 101 in binary. 

To read a binary number, you just read the 1s and 0s from the right to the left, just like we do with regular numbers. So, if you see 101, you read it as 1, 0, 1, and that means 5. 

In binary, you start from the rightmost digit and move to the left. Each digit has a different value, and the rightmost digit is always 1. So, in the binary number 101, the rightmost digit is 1, and that means 1. The next digit is 0, and that means 2. And the next digit is 1, and that means 4. When you add them all together, you get 5. 

So, binary is like a special way to write numbers using only two letters: 1 and 0. It's easy to read and write because you just have to read the digits from the right to the left and start from the rightmost digit, which is always 1.

[Original Text]
Binary is a special way of writing numbers using only two digits: 0 and 1. It's like having two different colored blocks: red and blue. In binary, you use these blocks to make numbers.

To read or write binary, you just write down the 1s and 0s in a row. For example, the number 5 in binary is written as 101.

When you read a binary number, you start from the rightmost digit. This is called the least significant bit (LSB). You count to the leftmost digit, which is the most significant bit (MSB).

For example, in the binary number 101, the LSB is 1, the next bit is 0, and the MSB is 1. So, when you read 101, you start from the rightmost 1, which is the LSB, and then count to the leftmost 1, which is the MSB.

When you write a binary number, you start from the leftmost digit, which is the MSB, and then move to the rightmost digit, which is the LSB.

So, to read or write binary, you just start from the rightmost digit and move to the leftmost digit. It's like counting from the right to the left, but instead of using numbers, you use 1s and 0s!

Non-Watermarked Text Detection: True Negative

[Watermarked Text]
Yes, you should consider making more conservative investments in your company 401(K) if you're going to leave your job in a couple of years. Here's why:

1. **Maximize Impact Value**: More conservative investments, such as low-cost index funds, will allow you to take advantage of the 401(K) matching contributions and limit your tax liability. This will help you maximize the impact value of your 401(K) when you leave.

2. **Minimize Risk**: By choosing more conservative investments, you'll reduce the risk of market volatility, which can harm your 401(K) balance.

3. **Avoid Penalties**: Conservative investments can help you avoid penalties for withdrawing funds from your 401(K) before the plan is vested.

4. **Reduce Tax Liability**: More conservative investments can help reduce your tax liability, especially if you're in a high tax bracket.

While conservative investments may not yield the same returns as more aggressive investments, they can provide a more stable and predictable growth trajectory for your 401(K). However, it's essential to consult with a financial advisor before making any investment decisions to ensure that your investment strategy aligns with your financial goals and risk tolerance.

[Original Text]
If you're planning to leave your job in a couple of years, making more conservative investments in your company 401(K) can be a good strategy. Here's why:

1. **Maximize tax benefits**: By investing in conservative funds, you'll maximize the tax benefits of your 401(K) contributions. This means you'll get a larger tax deduction, which can be helpful in reducing your overall tax bill.
2. **Minimize investment risk**: Conservative investments tend to be less volatile than more aggressive ones, which can help you minimize your investment risk.
3. **Reduce transaction costs**: If you're leaving your job in a couple of years, you may not have the flexibility to change your investments frequently. By investing in conservative funds, you'll avoid the transaction costs associated with frequent investment changes.
4. **Maintain account balance**: Conservative investments can help you maintain a higher balance in your 401(K) account, which can be beneficial if you need to access your funds quickly.
5. **Avoid tax-loss harvesting**: By investing in conservative funds, you'll avoid the potential for tax-loss harvesting, which can be beneficial if you're leaving your job in a couple of years.

Overall, making more conservative investments in your company 401(K) can help you maximize your tax benefits, minimize investment risk, and reduce transaction costs, all while maintaining your account balance and avoiding potential tax-loss harvesting.

Perplexity (WM): 5.8763 | Perplexity (No WM): 6.1329
Watermarked Text Detection: False Negative
Non-Watermarked Text Detection: True Negative

[Watermarked Text]
Certainly! Vitel toné is a popular Uruguayan dish that combines tender strips of beef with a flavorful broth. Here's a simple recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 2 pounds beef, cut into 2-inch strips
- 1 large onion, chopped
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 2 bay leaves
- 2 tablespoons tomato paste
- 2 cups beef broth
- 2 cups red wine
- 2 cups water
- 2 tablespoons olive oil
- 2 tablespoons cornstarch
- 2 bay leaves
- 2 tablespoons olive oil

Instructions:
1. In a large pot, heat the olive oil over medium heat.
2. Add the chopped onion and garlic, sauté until softened.
3. Add the beef strips and cook until browned on all sides.
4. Add the tomato paste and cook for 1 minute.
5. Add the beef broth, red wine, and water. Bring to a boil.
6. Reduce the heat to low, add the bay leaves, and simmer for 30 minutes or until the beef is tender.
7. Add the cornstarch and cook until thickened.
8. Remove the bay leaves and serve with steamed rice or bread.

This recipe will yield about 4-6 servings. You can adjust the ingredients based on your taste preferences. Enjoy your Vitel toné!

[Original Text]
Vitel toné is a traditional Uruguayan dish made with stew beef, potatoes, and vegetables. Here's a simple recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 2 lbs beef chuck roast, cut into chunks
- 4 large potatoes, peeled and chopped
- 2 carrots, chopped
- 2 celery stalks, chopped
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 2 tbsp olive oil
- 1 cup beef broth
- 1/2 cup red wine
- 1/2 cup white wine
- Salt and pepper to taste

Instructions:
1. Preheat your oven to 375°F (190°C).
2. In a large skillet, heat the olive oil over medium heat. Add the beef chunks and cook until browned, about 5-7 minutes.
3. Add the chopped potatoes, carrots, celery, and garlic to the skillet. Cook for 2-3 minutes.
4. Pour in the beef broth, red wine, and white wine. Stir to combine.
5. Transfer the mixture to a baking dish and cover with aluminum foil.
6. Bake in the preheated oven for 2-3 hours, or until the beef is tender and the vegetables are tender.
7. Season with salt and pepper to taste.
8. Serve hot with crusty bread or rice.

Enjoy your Uruguayan vitel toné!

Watermarked Text Detection: False Negative
Non-Watermarked Text Detection: True Negative

[Watermarked Text]
Opor Ayam is a popular Indonesian dish made with chicken and spices. Here's a simple recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 2 whole chicken breasts, cut into small pieces
- 2 tablespoons vegetable oil
- 2 medium onions, chopped
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 1 teaspoon ginger, grated
- 2 tablespoons soy sauce
- 2 tablespoons brown sugar
- 1 teaspoon turmeric powder
- 1 teaspoon turmeric
- 1 teaspoon chili powder
- 2 cups water
- 2 bay leaves, chopped
- 2 stalks of celery, chopped
- 2 stalks of carrots, chopped
- 2 stalks of celery, chopped

Instructions:
1. Heat the oil in a large pot over medium heat.
2. Add the onions, garlic, and ginger and cook until softened.
3. Add the chicken pieces and cook until browned.
4. Add the soy sauce, brown sugar, turmeric powder, turmeric, and chili powder.
5. Add the water, bay leaves, celery, carrots, and celery.
6. Bring the mixture to a boil, then reduce the heat and simmer for 30-40 minutes, or until the chicken is tender.
7. Remove the bay leaves and serve the Opor Ayam hot.

You can adjust the amount of oil, spices, and vegetables to your taste. Enjoy!

[Original Text]
Opor Ayam is a popular Indonesian dish made with chicken, vegetables, and spices. Here's a simple recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 1 whole chicken, cut into pieces
- 1 large onion, chopped
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 1 bell pepper, sliced
- 1 green onion, sliced
- 1/2 cup vegetable oil
- 1/2 cup coconut milk
- 1/4 cup chicken broth
- 1/4 cup brown sugar
- 1/4 cup tomato paste
- 1/2 teaspoon salt
- 1/4 teaspoon black pepper
- 1/4 teaspoon ground coriander
- 1/4 teaspoon ground cumin
- 1/4 teaspoon ground cinnamon
- 1/4 teaspoon ground nutmeg
- 1/4 teaspoon ground cloves

Instructions:
1. In a large pot, heat the oil over medium heat.
2. Add the onion, garlic, bell pepper, and green onion and cook until softened.
3. Add the chicken and cook until browned.
4. Add the coconut milk, chicken broth, brown sugar, tomato paste, salt, black pepper, coriander, cumin, cinnamon, nutmeg, and cloves.
5. Bring to a boil, then reduce heat and simmer for 20-25 minutes.
6. Remove from heat and serve hot. Enjoy!

Prompt: You are a helpful assistant, please answer the following question within 300 words:
What do the different octane values of gasoline mean?
Perplexity (WM): 8.0946 | Perplexity (No WM): 5.4615
Watermarked Text Detection: True Positive
Non-Watermarked Text Detection: True Negative

[Watermarked Text]
The octane value of gasoline is a measure of its ability to resist detonation during combustion. Detonation occurs when the mixture of fuel and air in the engine burns too quickly, causing the engine to misfire and potentially damage it. The octane value is measured using a device called an octane tester, which simulates the conditions of the engine and evaluates the fuel's ability to burn smoothly.

Higher octane values, such as 87, 91, or 95, indicate that the fuel is more resistant to detonation. In contrast, lower octane values, such as 71 or 74, suggest that the fuel is more prone to detonation. The octane rating is typically printed on the gasoline container and is an important consideration for the reliability and performance of gasoline engines.

In general, gasoline with higher octane values are better suited for high-performance engines, such as those found in sports or racing vehicles, while lower octane values are more suitable for standard engines. However, it's essential to note that higher octane values can also increase the cost of gasoline, as the production of high-octane gasoline is more complex and energy-intensive.

In summary, the octane value of gasoline is a measure of its ability to resist detonation during combustion. Higher octane values indicate greater resistance to detonation, making the fuel better suited for high-performance engines, but can also increase the cost of gasoline.

[Original Text]
The octane value of gasoline is a measure of its resistance to engine knocking, also known as detonation. Knocking occurs when the combustion process in an engine happens too quickly, causing the engine to vibrate and produce a knocking sound. The octane value of gasoline is measured in a laboratory setting, and it is based on a series of tests that measure the fuel's ability to withstand detonation.

The octane value of gasoline is typically expressed as a percentage, with a range of 70 to 100. A higher octane value means that the fuel is more resistant to detonation, and it is better suited for high-performance engines. A lower octane value means that the fuel is more prone to detonation, and it is better suited for lower-powered engines.

In general, gasoline with a higher octane value is better suited for engines with high compression ratios, such as those found in high-performance cars, motorcycles, and trucks. This is because these engines are more susceptible to detonation, and a higher octane value can help prevent knocking and potential engine damage.

In summary, the octane value of gasoline is a measure of its resistance to engine knocking, and a higher octane value means that the fuel is more resistant to detonation, making it better suited for high-performance engines. A lower octane value, on the other hand, is more prone to detonation, making it better suited for lower-powered engines.

[Original Text]
Fixing a leaking roof can be a daunting task, but with the right tools and techniques, it can be done efficiently. First, identify the source of the leak by looking for signs of moisture, such as wet spots, water stains, or water droplets on the roof. Next, determine the cause of the leak, such as damaged shingles, gutters, or skylights. Once you have identified the issue, gather the necessary tools, including a sturdy ladder, roofing nails, cement, and a roofing hammer.

If the leak is due to a clogged gutter, clean it out and replace if necessary. If the leak is caused by a damaged skylight, replace it with a new one.

After fixing the leak, seal the area with roofing cement to prevent water intrusion. Apply the cement to the edges of the shingles, gutters, and skylights to create a tight seal.

Finally, inspect the roof for any other issues that may have contributed to the leak. Make any necessary repairs or upgrades to the roof to prevent future leaks.

- 2 onions, chopped
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 2 bay leaves
- 1 tsp cumin
- 1 tsp paprika
- 1 tsp black pepper

[Original Text]
Milanesa de peceto is a traditional Argentinian dish that's sure to impress your guests. Here's a recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 1 lb ground beef
- 1 cup breadcrumbs
- 1/2 cup chopped onions
- 1/2 cup chopped parsley
- 1 tsp garlic powder
- 1 tsp paprika
- 1 tsp salt
- 1 tsp black pepper
- 1 tbsp olive oil
- 1 cup shredded cheese
- 1 cup sour cream
- 1/2 cup chopped cilantro

Instructions:
1. In a large bowl, combine the ground beef, breadcrumbs, onions, parsley, garlic powder, paprika, salt, and black pepper. Mix well.
2. In a large skillet, heat the olive oil over medium heat. Add the beef mixture and cook until browned, about 5-7 minutes.
3. Remove the skillet from heat and let it rest for a few minutes.
4. In a separate bowl, mix the shredded cheese, sour cream, and cilantro.
5. Pour the cheese mixture over the cooked beef mixture in the skillet.
6. Cover the skillet with aluminum foil and bake in the oven at 375°F (190°C) for 25-30 minutes, or until the cheese is melted.

Enjoy your homemade Milanesa de peceto!

What happens to the data deleted on our computers / phones / devices ? I always assumed the device just farted out the data we deleted . I am curious as to what exactly happens . How is it removed from our devices ? Please explain like I'm five.
Perplexity (WM): 11.8680 | Perplexity (No WM): 8.9441
Watermarked Text Detection: True Positive
Non-Watermarked Text Detection: True Negative

[Watermarked Text]
When you delete something on your computer or phone, it doesn’t just magically disappear. Instead, the device performs a special process to remove the deleted data. The data is stored in a place called the hard drive, which is like a big box inside your device. When you delete something, the device tells the box to free up the space where it was stored.

The hard drive then makes sure the deleted data is completely erased by using a process called "wiping." Wiping means setting the space where the deleted data was stored to a random value, so it can't be read again. It’s like covering the data with a blanket, so someone else can't find it.

Once the data is wiped, the device cleans out the space where it was stored, so that new data can be added there. If you try to find the deleted data later, the device won't be able to read it because it has been completely erased.

So, when you delete something, the device doesn't just " fart out" the data; it makes sure it’s completely removed and ready for new information.

[Original Text]
When you delete data from your computer or phone, it's not like it just "farts out" or disappears. Instead, the data is physically erased from the device's storage medium, such as a hard drive, SSD, or memory.

Here's what actually happens:

1. **Data relocation**: When you delete data, the device's operating system moves the deleted data to a special area called the " recycle bin" or "trash." This area is like a temporary storage space where the data is kept until you decide to empty it.

2. **Erasure process**: When you empty the recycle bin, the operating system initiates a process called "erasure." This process involves rewriting the storage medium to overwrite the deleted data with random bits, effectively erasing it.

3. **Physical erasure**: The physical erasure process can vary depending on the type of storage medium. For example, on a hard drive, the erasure process might involve rewriting the magnetic tracks that store the data. On an SSD, the erasure process might involve rewriting the flash memory cells that store the data.

4. **Data loss**: Once the erasure process is complete, the deleted data is no longer available on the device. It's truly gone, and it can't be recovered easily.

So, when you delete data, it's not just "farted out"; it's physically erased from the device's storage medium.

The chemical composition of Kevlar is a combination of carbon, nitrogen, and oxygen atoms arranged in a specific structure. Kevlar, also known as Kevlar fabrics, is a synthetic fiber made from a polyamide polymer. It is a type of aramid, which means it is composed of aramid polymers. The chemical structure of Kevlar is a combination of two monomers: polyamidoacetamide (PAAM) and polyamidoacetophenone (PAP). These monomers are linked together through a process called polycondensation, where they form a long chain of repeating chemical units.

The repeating units in Kevlar are typically composed of a nitrogen atom bonded to two carbon atoms and an oxygen atom, followed by a carbon atom bonded to two nitrogen atoms and an oxygen atom. This structure gives Kevlar its exceptional strength, stiffness, and resistance to abrasion, making it ideal for a wide range of applications, including protective gear, outdoor clothing, and industrial equipment.

Kevlar is then spun through a process called electrospinning, where the polymer is drawn through a small hole using an electric field. This process creates a strong, hollow fiber. Kevlar is also known for its lightweight nature, which allows it to be woven into various fabrics, such as clothing, helmets, and vests.

In summary, Kevlar is made out of a polyamide polymer, a type of aramid, and is known for its exceptional strength, stiffness, and resistance to abrasion, making it ideal for various applications.

The "exit node" is the last person in the chain who sends the information to the website. It's like the last person in your secret message chain who sends the message to your friend.

So, when you use the Tor network to visit a website, your information is passed through a series of Tor nodes, one by one, until it reaches the exit node, which then sends it to the website. This helps to keep your information safe and private, and no one person can see it.

Hi, I'd like to learn to bake challah. Can you give me a recipe for that?
Perplexity (WM): 5.5978 | Perplexity (No WM): 4.5618
Watermarked Text Detection: True Positive
Non-Watermarked Text Detection: False Positive

[Watermarked Text]
Sure! Challah is a traditional Jewish bread that's often baked during holidays and special occasions. Here's a basic recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 2 cups all-purpose flour
- 2 tablespoons salt
- 2 tablespoons sugar
- 1/2 cup active dry yeast
- 1/2 cup milk
- 2 tablespoons vegetable oil
- 2 tablespoons eggs
- 2 tablespoons lemon juice
- 2 tablespoons honey
- 2 tablespoons vanilla extract

Instructions:
1. Preheat the oven to 375°F (190°C).
2. In a large mixing bowl, combine the flour, salt, sugar, and yeast.
3. Add the milk, vegetable oil, eggs, lemon juice, honey, and vanilla extract. Mix until a dough forms.
4. Let the dough rise in a warm place for 1 hour, or until it doubles in size.
5. Punch down the dough and shape it into a round loaf.
6. Place the loaf in a greased loaf pan and let it rise again for 30 minutes.
7. Bake the loaf in the preheated oven for 50-60 minutes, or until it's golden brown.
8. Let the bread cool for a few minutes before slicing it into pieces.

Remember to keep the yeast fresh and follow the recipe carefully for best results. Happy baking!

[Original Text]
Sure, here's a basic recipe for challah:

Ingredients:
- 3 cups all-purpose flour
- 1/2 cup water
- 1/4 cup vegetable oil
- 1/2 teaspoon salt
- 1/2 teaspoon sugar
- 1/2 teaspoon yeast
- 1/2 teaspoon milk
- 1/2 teaspoon vanilla extract

Instructions:
1. Preheat your oven to 350°F (175°C).
2. In a large mixing bowl, combine the flour, salt, sugar, yeast, milk, and vanilla extract.
3. In a separate bowl, mix the water and vegetable oil.
4. Add the wet ingredients to the dry ingredients and mix until a dough forms.
5. Place the dough in a clean bowl, cover with a damp cloth, and let it rise in a warm place for 1-2 hours until it doubles in size.
6. Punch down the dough and shape it into a circle.
7. Place the dough on a parchment-lined baking sheet.
8. Let the dough rise for another 30 minutes.
9. Bake the challah for 45-60 minutes, or until it's golden brown and puffed up.
10. Let the challah cool for a few minutes before slicing it into rounds and serving with melted butter.

Enjoy your homemade challah!

Ingredients:
- 200g pork belly
- 200g mushrooms
- 2-inch pieces of ginger
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 2 cups water
- 2 tablespoons soy sauce
- 2 tablespoons oyster sauce
- 2 tablespoons vegetable oil
- 2 green onions, thinly sliced

Instructions:

Tong Sui, also known as Sweet and Sour Pork, is a popular Hong Kong dish that's sure to impress your guests. Here's a simple recipe to get you started:

Ingredients:
- 500g pork belly, cut into chunks
- 2 cups soy sauce
- 1 cup rice vinegar
- 1 cup honey
- 1 tablespoon sesame oil
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 1 inch ginger, minced
- 2 green onions, chopped
- 1 red pepper, thinly sliced (optional)

Instructions:
1. In a large bowl, combine the soy sauce, rice vinegar, honey, and sesame oil.
2. In a pan, cook the pork belly chunks in sesame oil until browned.
3. Add the garlic and ginger, and cook for 1 minute.
4. Add the pork chunks to the pan, and cook for 5-7 minutes, or until cooked through.
5. Pour the sauce mixture over the pork, and cook for 2-3 minutes.
6. Add the green onions and red pepper (if using), and cook for 1-2 minutes.
7. Serve the Tong Sui hot, garnished with additional green onions and sesame seeds.

This recipe is easy to make, and the sweet and sour flavors will add a unique and delicious twist to your dish. Enjoy cooking and impress your guests!

2. **Emergency Funds**: If you need quick cash for an emergency, a loan can provide the necessary funds without depleting your savings.

3. **Student Loans**: When pursuing higher education, a loan can help cover tuition, books, and other expenses, providing financial relief while building student debt.

4. **Business Loans**: For entrepreneurs, a loan can help finance business operations, expansion, and growth, without having to dip into personal savings.

5. **Personal Loans**: If you're facing a financial crunch, a personal loan can provide temporary relief to cover essential expenses.

6. **Mortgage Loans**: If you're buying a home, a loan can help pay the down payment and make monthly mortgage payments, spreading out the cost over time.

7. **Small Loans**: For small purchases like furniture or tools, a loan can help cover the cost without depleting your savings.

8. **Loan Consolid**: If you're already in debt, consolidating loans into a single loan can lower interest rates and simplify monthly payments.

In these situations, considering a loan can provide financial flexibility, reduce financial strain, and simplify your repayment process.

[Original Text]
Human blood is typically red due to the presence of hemoglobin, a protein in red blood cells that carries oxygen. However, there are rare cases where blood can appear other colors, such as blue, green, or even black.

Blue blood is a result of a genetic disorder called methemoglobinemia, where the body produces an abnormal form of hemoglobin called methemoglobin. This condition occurs when methemoglobin binds to oxygen, causing the blood to appear blue.

Green blood is extremely rare and is a result of a genetic disorder called sulfhemoglobinemia. This condition occurs when the body produces a abnormal form of hemoglobin called sulfhemoglobin, which does not bind to oxygen. As a result, the blood may appear green.

Black blood is also extremely rare and is a result of a genetic disorder called methemoglobinemia. This condition occurs when the body produces an abnormal form of hemoglobin called methemoglobin, which does not bind to oxygen. As a result, the blood may appear black.

It's essential to note that these conditions are extremely rare and often require medical attention. If you notice someone's blood is an unusual color, it's crucial to seek medical attention immediately.

Pearls are small, round gemstones formed by the shell of a mollusk in response to an irritant, such as a piece of sand or a parasite, entering the mollusk's shell. The mollusk secretes layers of nacre (a type of calcium carbonate) around the irritant, forming a pearl.

There are two types of pearls: natural and cultured. Natural pearls are extremely rare and valuable, while cultured pearls are more common and affordable. Cultured pearls are created by inserting a small piece of shell or tissue into a mollusk's shell, which then prompts the mollusk to produce a pearl.

Pearls can be found in various regions around the world, including:

1. Japan: Japan is the largest producer of cultured pearls in the world, particularly the Akaha and Akoya pearls.
2. South Australia: South Australia is another significant producer of cultured pearls, particularly the Tahitian pearl.
3. China: China is a major producer of cultured pearls, particularly the Akoya pearl.
4. Philippines: Philippines is a significant producer of cultured pearls, particularly the Akoya pearl.
5. Indonesia: Indonesia is a significant producer of cultured pearls, particularly the Akoya pearl.
6. Mexico: Mexico is a significant producer of cultured pearls, particularly the Akoya pearl.

These are just a few examples of the many regions around the world where pearls are found, produced, and sold.

[Original Text]
Pearls are small, round, and often shiny objects that form inside a mollusk. They are created when an irritant, such as a piece of shell or a parasite, enters the mollusk and causes it to secrete layers of nacre, a type of calcium carbonate, to cover the irritant. Over time, these layers build up to form a pearl. Pearls are typically found in various types of mollusks, such as oysters, mussels, and clams. The most common type of pearl is the Akoya pearl, which is found in Japanese oysters. Other types of pearls, such as Tahitian and South Sea pearls, are found in different types of mollusks. Pearls are also found in freshwater pearls, which are formed in freshwater mussels.
